## `AFG-4782-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Thread-Counter Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Stall Apparel Fiber Content Label & National Safety Class
